# Generated Output Cleanup and Input Retention

Historical generated compiler artifacts, package caches, runtime fixture outputs and command logs are disposable output. Before cleanup, preserved 1542 script/configuration inputs and Markdown reports (362102bytes) under `📥️inputs/retained-1016`. Existing ticket input/report folders were untouched. Excluded immutable tracked source copies from input retention to avoid duplicating repository source in the ticket. No compilation/test/runtime work was performed during this cleanup.

Retained names use bounded hash folders; all destination paths are at most256UTF16units. Reparse-point objects are removed before recursive generated-directory cleanup; their destinations are not traversed.

Generated-directory removal is the next closure action; final result is recorded in the master merge report.
